# 225: Chapter 228: Ogerpon’s Potential

Ogerpon's revenge was too brutal. Summer couldn't bear to watch after a while. The result? Pecharunt's two outer shells were shattered into several pieces, and its core was nearly split in half.

When its shell broke apart, the energy that leaked out naturally condensed into two shining purple crystals, bearing a striking resemblance to Tera crystals.

But Summer didn't have the time to investigate them yet. After sealing the half-dead Pecharunt inside a Poké Ball, he let out a long sigh.

Taking down Pecharunt had gone surprisingly smoothly—but dealing with the aftermath was a headache. Looking at the unconscious students and villagers, as well as the ones Carmine and Kieran's Pokémon had carried back, Summer sighed again. This Pecharunt had really stirred up a mess.

Then, things got even more complicated.

Just as Summer was wondering how to handle everything, Blueberry Academy's field trip leader, Briar, returned—right in time to witness the aftermath.

For a moment, Summer thought, Well… I'm screwed.

But to his surprise, she completely ignored the unconscious students.

Instead, she ran straight toward the glowing purple crystals, eyes wide with excitement. Checking her Rotom Phone one moment, examining the crystals the next, she was absolutely giddy—practically glowing with joy.

After learning about her true personality, Summer couldn't help but feel worried for these students' futures. This isn't a teacher… This is just a full-blown research fanatic!

In fact, she even offered to help with cleanup—just so she could study the crystals longer.

Since having extra hands made the job easier, Summer had no reason to refuse.

Briar proved to be a huge help, offering up the students' assigned lodging locations. Summer and Kieran carried the boys back, while Briar and Carmine handled the girls. The unconscious villagers were also returned to their homes.

By tomorrow morning, everyone would probably just think they fell asleep early and had some weird, vivid dreams.

By the time the cleanup was mostly finished, it was already past midnight. Briar immediately dove into her research, completely engrossed. Since Summer had already promised the crystals as payment, he didn't stop her.

Carmine stretched, yawning. "Man, our teacher's really something, huh?"

"Yaaawn~ Briar's alright, I guess," she said, rubbing her eyes. "But the second she gets into research mode, it's like she's possessed… Mm~! Still, I gotta say—this trip turned out way more fun than I expected~"

Despite the exhaustion, she was grinning ear to ear. The thrill of the battle, the chaos of the night, and getting to beat up those two annoying classmates—it had all been pretty exciting.

Kieran, however, looked uneasy. "Uh… Summer, the town square has security cameras. What if the administrator sees all this?"

Summer followed Kieran's gaze to the still-active surveillance cameras. Then he chuckled.

"No problem. I'll just tell him the truth tomorrow."

"Wait… what?!"

Summer smirked. "He personally fed those toxic mochi to the students. You think he's gonna go around telling people about it? Not a chance."

Carmine burst out laughing. "HAH! Summer, I never took you for the blackmailing type! Ooooh, just imagining the look on the old man's face… His last few beard hairs are so falling out!"

Kieran nodded. "Yeah! You've got, like… total crime boss energy right now!"

"…Do I?" Summer tilted his head. "Eh, whatever. The situation's settled now. You two should head home and get some rest. And don't worry about your grandparents—Hatterene checked on them. They're totally fine. Just exhausted. Might wake up with some sore muscles, though."

Carmine and Kieran nodded. "Alright. See ya tomorrow, Summer."

As they left, Summer turned his attention to Briar.

She was still completely absorbed in the glowing crystals.

"Oooh! Without a doubt, these are Poison Tera Crystals! They hold dense Tera energy and pure poison-type essence! But this unique energy signature… How fascinating!" Her eyes sparkled with excitement.

Summer cleared his throat. "Ahem. Briar-sensei, right?"

Briar glanced up, nodding absentmindedly. "That's me!"

Then, as if something clicked, she clapped her hands together in realization.

"Oooh, I know! You must be here to explain how these crystals formed, right?! I'd love to hear it! Maybe this could help me unravel the mystery of The Hidden Treasure of Area Zero!"

"…The what?"

Briar's eyes gleamed. "Yes! It's all connected! The truth behind Tera energy!"

She pulled a thick red book from her bag, flipping to an ancient, illustrated page. Holding it up to Summer, she let him read aloud:

"The Disk Pokémon. I have temporarily named it Terapagos. I hypothesize that the light emitted by Pokémon and their crystalline transformations originate from its body."

"…So this 'Hidden Treasure' is linked to Tera energy?"

Briar nodded enthusiastically. "Yes, exactly! My ancestor explored Area Zero and discovered Terapagos! I firmly believe it slumbers deep within the Great Crater of Paldea! Everyone dismissed his research as nonsense, but I will prove he was right!"

"…Wait. The Great Crater?"

Briar tilted her head. "Oh? Not from Paldea, huh? Then let me put it another way—do you know about the Great Pit?"

Summer's expression darkened.

The Great Pit. The same accursed pit that drove Paldea's kings mad with greed. The same pit that led to Ogerpon's suffering.

The same pit that the ancient king wanted to send Ogerpon into, hoping to plunder its riches.

"…Yeah. I know it."

Briar didn't seem to notice his change in tone.

"Mhm! In fact, Tera Orbs were invented using technology from the Great Pit! And my main reason for coming to Kitakami was to study the local Tera phenomena!"

"After all, the Tera energy here is exactly the same as in Paldea! Isn't that just so exciting?!"

Summer took a deep breath, then slowly exhaled. "Okay. So let me get this straight… If this Terapagos is the source of Tera energy, and it's sleeping in the Great Pit—then why is Kitakami experiencing Tera phenomena?"

Briar grinned. "Great question! The answer is simple—"

She raised a finger.

"The energy output and influence range are completely different. If there was a Terapagos as powerful as the one in Area Zero, half of Sinnoh and Johto would be covered in Tera phenomena! Now wouldn't that be amazing?"

"…Right."

"Oh! Speaking of which—Summer-kun, you still haven't told me where these Poison Tera Crystals came from!" Briar's eyes sparkled with curiosity.

Summer glanced down at the two purple crystals… and suddenly noticed something.

A line of text appeared, and the voice of his Ranger Assistant echoed in his mind.

[Dread Crystals: Formed from Pecharunt's fear and poison-type energy, mixed with the concentrated Tera energy gathered by Ogerpon's attacks.]

[Ogerpon's Potential: —— has changed.]

[New Potential: Tera Resonance. Ogerpon can imprint emotions onto Tera Crystals of different types. When it Terastallizes, items embedded with these crystals will resonate with Ogerpon, granting it unique Tera Forms.]

[Notes: 1. This ability only activates when Ogerpon believes the item and emotion are truly connected. 2. Ogerpon can also wield Tera Crystals imprinted with emotions from others.]

[Currently activated items: Teal Mask (Grass/Joy), Hearthflame Mask (Fire/Anger), Wellspring Mask (Water/Sorrow), Cornerstone Mask (Rock/Happiness).]

Summer's eyes widened in realization.

Finally… he understood the source of Ogerpon's power.

And if it could use crystals imprinted by others' emotions…

Then that meant—

Ogerpon could gain a brand-new mask.

A Poison Mask.

(End of Chapter)

---

# 226: Chapter 229: Pecharunt’s Past

The next morning, in the Kitakami Hall's office, the administrator wiped the cold sweat from his forehead as he watched the security footage of last night's chaos. If Summer hadn't stepped in, he didn't even want to imagine how disastrous the consequences could have been.

"This… I never expected such a dangerous Pokémon to be hiding in Verdant Town… I don't even know how to properly thank you, Lord Summer!"

Still dabbing at his sweat, the administrator's mind raced. Never mind the village's reputation—his own life had been at risk! Not only had the townsfolk and Blueberry Academy students been in actual danger, but he himself had even helped Pecharunt by distributing the toxic mochi!

How could I have been so blind? How did I not notice something was off?

But even worse—if Summer hadn't agreed to keep quiet about what happened, the administrator's life as he knew it would have been over.

Nope. No way. As soon as Summer leaves, I'm deleting this footage.

And then… I need to come up with a believable excuse for the students. They can't ever find out about this…

Summer, meanwhile, remained composed. "Administrator, my contract still has a few days left. I'll be staying in Kitakami for a bit longer to finish my assigned work."

"Stay as long as you'd like!" The administrator forced a grin before hesitating. "…Wait. There aren't any more toxic mochi left, are there?"

"There shouldn't be. Pecharunt reabsorbed the scattered energy in order to fight me."

Summer stood up. "If there's nothing else, I'll take my leave."

The administrator quickly sprang up, rushing ahead to open the door for him. "No, no, no! Absolutely nothing at all, sir!"

As soon as Summer left, the administrator shut the door behind him and let out a huge sigh of relief.

Why does Kitakami keep running into such big problems lately?

First, the Loyal Three revived. Then, Ogerpon was cleared of blame after centuries. And now, a Pokémon that could control people nearly turned the village into a disaster zone…

Could there be even more dangers lurking in this village that I don't know about…?

—

"Hey! Over here, Summer!"

As Summer stepped outside, he spotted Carmine waving enthusiastically. Kieran was beside her, raising a hand in a smaller, more reserved greeting.

Summer approached them. "How are your grandparents doing?"

Carmine shrugged. "Grandma's totally fine. Grandpa's back's acting up again, though. She's staying home to take care of him."

"And your classmates?"

"No real issues, surprisingly," Kieran said, thinking it over. "Maybe it's just 'cause they're younger?"

"As long as they're fine, that's good. By the way… Where's your teacher?"

Last night, after Summer had told Briar about the emotions imprinted on the Tera crystals, she had suddenly gotten way too excited. She clapped her hands together, shouting something about "Everything makes sense now!" and how emotions were the missing factor in Tera energy fluctuations.

Then, before Summer could even react, she thanked him profusely… and vanished into the night.

Who knew where she ran off to, or what she was doing?

Carmine sighed. "Briar-sensei? Yeah, uh… She went back to Blueberry Academy."

Summer blinked. "Wait, what? She just left you guys here?!"

"She is coming back," Carmine said dryly. "She said she'd return by the end of the trip. And guess what? She put me in charge while she's gone."

Carmine groaned, rubbing her temples. "I dunno if it's 'cause I helped stop the disaster or just 'cause I live here, but she was way too trusting about it…"

Kieran patted her shoulder, grinning. "Hey, look at the bright side—congrats on your promotion!"

Carmine slowly turned to face him, grabbing his shoulders with an eerily calm smile.

"Oh, Kieran," she said sweetly. "You're not getting out of this. Briar also put you in charge—she just told me to tell you since you don't have a phone."

Kieran froze.

"Wait—what?! But I wanted to battle Ogerpon today!"

"Too bad, Assistant Manager Kieran!" Carmine cackled, dragging him away. "We've got responsibilities now!"

"NOOOOOOO—"

Summer watched the two siblings disappear into the distance, shaking his head.

With the immediate crisis resolved, he could finally turn his attention to Pecharunt.

It definitely knew valuable information. Most importantly—the whereabouts of the man who had traveled with Ogerpon in the past.

This was his chance to give Ogerpon the closure it deserved.

—

A quiet hillside.

Pecharunt sat there, surrounded by all-too-familiar faces.

It was confused.

It should have been terrified.

After all, these were the ones who had defeated and humiliated it. And now that it was in human captivity, they would surely torment it—just like it had tormented others.

And yet…

It felt calm.

Summer didn't speak immediately. Instead, he observed the glowing text that appeared around Pecharunt.

[Pecharunt – Poison/Ghost-Type Pokémon]

[Ability: Toxic Puppet]

[Potentials: Overclocked Poison (Inactive), Parasitic Control (Inactive), Treasure Sense (Active)]

[Overclocked Poison: Those poisoned by Pecharunt's toxins temporarily gain enhanced abilities—but at the cost of exhausting their own life force. It also amplifies their most deeply craved desires.]

[Parasitic Control: Those poisoned by Pecharunt's toxins fall under its control. If linked via Toxic Chains, Pecharunt can share in the target's power-ups and status boosts.]

[Note: If Pecharunt creates Toxic Chains that detach from its body, the control becomes permanent. These chains permanently alter the target's form. However, if a target breaks free, Pecharunt suffers severe backlash.]

Summer's eyes narrowed.

So that's how Pecharunt's powers worked…

This explained everything—how it had controlled others, why the Loyal Three had transformed, and even why it had become weaker after they rebelled.

It had suffered from the backlash of losing them.

But now, its two main abilities were completely inactive.

It had lost all its power to control others.

Was it because of those two purple crystals?

Was Pecharunt's own fear enough to cripple its abilities?

Then, Summer turned to its remaining ability.

[Treasure Sense: Reacts to valuable objects—the greater the value, the stronger the reaction. However, it also causes an overwhelming obsession with hoarding treasures.]

Summer exhaled.

So that's why it stole Ogerpon's masks.

To Pecharunt, they must have been treasures of immeasurable worth.

If that was the case…

Had it come all the way to Kitakami just for those masks?

"…Alright, Pecharunt." Summer's gaze locked onto the trembling Pokémon. "Time to start talking."

He nodded to Hatterene, activating the psychic link for translation.

Pecharunt hesitated. "Peach?" (Talk about what…?)

Summer's voice was firm.

"Start from the beginning. Tell me everything about the masks you stole."

Ogerpon's expression turned serious—it needed to know what had happened after it left. What had become of the man who was like a father to it?

Pecharunt had originally planned to keep its mouth shut.

But now, with its deepest fears stripped away, the chains that once bound its heart were gone. Its face was unnervingly calm as it finally began to recount the events of the past—along with its own origin.

Pecharunt was born from the twisted desires of a Paldean king—willed into existence by his wish upon the fabled Wishing Tree.

If Ogerpon had been gifted a kaleidoscope of emotions by the man who raised it, then Pecharunt had been forged from only two feelings, endlessly spiraling into corruption.

The first was an obsessive need for control.

Its master, the king, lived in fear—fear of losing his power, fear of his people rising against him. To mask his own cowardice, he ruled with an iron fist, enforcing a brutal regime that bound the entire empire under his command.

Pecharunt's ability to manipulate others through its toxins was a direct manifestation of this fear.

The second was insatiable greed.

The king's craving for wealth and treasures fueled Pecharunt's own unnatural compulsion to hoard valuables. This obsession would later drive a wedge between them—ultimately leading to Pecharunt's betrayal.

When the king first discovered Pecharunt's abilities on the remote island, he had been ecstatic.

With this power, he could create an army of unconditionally loyal warriors.

He also noticed that the newborn Pokémon was highly interested in his extravagant jewelry.

So, he struck a deal—he would shower Pecharunt with riches, and in return, Pecharunt would use its poison to bind warriors to his service.

Upon returning to Paldea, the king—normally a stingy hoarder—generously bestowed treasures upon Pecharunt, and in exchange, Pecharunt created an elite battalion of poisoned warriors.

The king was thrilled.

But with each passing day, his joy began to erode…

And his fear grew.

One fateful day, the king bore witness to an experiment that finally shattered his trust.

He stood before an entire town's population—enslaved, reduced to puppets under Pecharunt's control.

And Pecharunt, staring up at him, demanded even more treasure.

The horrifying truth dawned upon him.

As long as he could keep Pecharunt satisfied, the poison would remain under his command.

But what if one day, he could no longer provide enough?

Would his own people turn against him? Would his entire empire become Pecharunt's to control?

The king, pale-faced and trembling, feigned agreement. He promised to fetch more treasures from the royal palace.

But in reality—he branded the entire town as "rebels" and ordered his army to slaughter every last one of them.

Pecharunt had been terrified.

Its very nature was built upon fear—and now, it had turned against it.

The army had been ordered to hunt it down.

With its nimble movements and clever tricks, Pecharunt barely managed to escape. But the betrayal left it scarred with a deep-rooted paranoia.

Humans could not be trusted.

They lied. They broke promises. They slaughtered their own without hesitation.

Pecharunt made a decision.

If it couldn't trust humans, it would control the simple-minded Pokémon instead.

It fled to a remote mountain range, remaining in hiding as it perfected the creation of its Toxic Chains.

After much searching, it finally found three ideal targets.

The trio were weak, insecure, and full of unchecked desires.

Most importantly—they had the potential to be powerful.

Using toxic mochi and its Toxic Chains, Pecharunt transformed the three into its most loyal subordinates.

With the Loyal Three under its command, Pecharunt began amassing an enormous fortune—stealing from passing merchants, plundering treasures from distant lands.

Then, one day, Pecharunt overheard rumors of the mystical masks of Kitakami.

The moment it heard about them, its Treasure Sense surged.

These masks were valuable beyond anything it had encountered before.

Fueled by overconfidence from its past successes, Pecharunt made a bold decision.

It would travel to Kitakami itself and claim these treasures for its hoard.

From there, the events were ones Summer already knew.

The Loyal Three invaded Oni Mountain, stealing the masks from the man who protected them with his life.

In the end, only the Teal Mask remained—saved by the man's desperate efforts.

But what about Pecharunt's side of the story?

In truth—Pecharunt had never even seen Ogerpon before.

It had been too arrogant, too sure of itself.

It had merely commanded the Loyal Three to carry out the heist, never once considering that anything could go wrong.

Then—the man spoke.

"You… you're Pecharunt?! I never thought I'd see the day… A Pokémon born from King Felipe I's greed… Has the royal family finally found a way to control you?"

Felipe I was the very king of Paldea who once led the campaign to exterminate Pecharunt. Recalling that bloody experience, Pecharunt was utterly terrified, momentarily losing control of its three companions and fleeing aimlessly in a panic.

Seeing that the three companions had stopped moving, the man chose to pursue Pecharunt instead.

Pecharunt had no idea what the man's intentions were, but in its panic, it suddenly heard a voice from the mountaintop calling out to it. Clinging to this as if it were a lifeline, it rushed toward the source of the voice at full speed.

It only remembered that the voice at the mountaintop instructed it to unleash its power. Obeying the command, Pecharunt did as it was told, and the crystal at the bottom of the Crystal Pool erupted with a dazzling light. A thick mist spread out in all directions.

By the time Pecharunt came back to its senses, the man had disappeared without a trace. Now calm, it quickly used its connection to the poisonous chains to locate its three companions.

Pecharunt then arrived at the village at the foot of the mountain, only to witness its three companions holding up a mask and laughing wildly. Just as it was about to regain control over them, Ogerpon, wearing the Teal Mask, descended from the mountain.

Overcome with rage, Ogerpon underwent a unique Terastallization and, wielding a thorned club, viciously beat the three companions. For some reason, the Terastallized attack traveled along the poisonous chains and struck Pecharunt itself.

Ignoring the outer shell's defense, the attack pierced straight into its core. After enduring the fatal attacks that took down its three companions, Pecharunt lost consciousness and fell into a dormant state.

Perhaps because of its somewhat endearing appearance, a villager picked it up. Over time, it was passed down through generations, eventually becoming the heirloom doll of Granny Ade's family, sleeping within the Ade store.

Its partial awakening was triggered by the revival of its three companions. Albert's greed, no less than that of the King of Paldea, resonated with Pecharunt, causing it to swell with ambition once more, hatching a plan to control the villagers.

However, the betrayal of its three companions drove it into a fury. Though the backlash from this betrayal weakened its condition, it ironically helped facilitate its complete revival, allowing it to execute its long-devised scheme.

As for what happened next, Summer knew all too well—because he had experienced it firsthand. The question was, had Pecharunt spoken any lies?

"Hatterene."

Hatterene understood Summer's intent and shook its head. Everything Pecharunt had just said was true.

Through Pecharunt's words, the hazy memories of the three companions suddenly became clear.

They recalled that the battle scars in the Forbidden Cavern were not the result of an external fight. Instead, after breaking free from Pecharunt's control, they had been overwhelmed by their own intense desires and fought among themselves over the masks.

Ironically, the brawl helped them regain some clarity and planted the idea of escaping Pecharunt's control. After a brief discussion, they each took a mask and fled to the village at the foot of the mountain.

As for why one mask was left behind, it was simply because none of them wanted the others to gain an advantage, so they reached a compromise.

"Summer, they're telling the truth too."

Hatterene's voice echoed in Summer's mind, and he wore a thoughtful expression. "So that means Ogerpon's companion wasn't actually harmed? He disappeared because of the thick mist from the Crystal Pool?"

Beside him, Ogerpon looked guilty. If its companions hadn't harmed the man, then perhaps it had been too heavy-handed with its retaliation.

But… it was a great relief that the man hadn't been hurt. Someone as kind as him should never have suffered.

"No, Ogerpon, I know you're a good kid. You don't have to feel guilty," Summer reassured it with a gentle pat on the head before shifting the conversation.

"In the end, Pecharunt, you've never done a single good deed, have you? Controlling others, committing robbery, causing last night's chaos… It's time for some re-education."

Pecharunt had lost all its arrogance, quietly accepting reality. Without that twisted obsession with control, its personality and thoughts no longer seemed as warped.

"But still, we never found out what happened to Ogerpon's companion. What exactly occurred within the mist of the Crystal Pool? Looks like we'll need to investigate it."

Back then, Pecharunt had used its power to create the mist. Since its power stemmed from fear, did that mean it was fueled by emotions?

Summer had a new theory. If that were the case, then perhaps Ogerpon's special Terastallization could also trigger the mist at the Crystal Pool.

Maybe they would soon uncover the truth hidden within the fog—

And the true whereabouts of the man who once traveled with Ogerpon.

(End of Chapter)

# 227: Chapter 230:  Reunion and Farewell

The Crystal Pool, a natural spring located at the peak of Oni Mountain, served as the source of the entire Kitakami river system and provided drinking water for the residents of Mossui Town. Because of this, outsiders were theoretically prohibited from entering the area.

However, given Summer's current relationship with the caretaker, the moment he made a call, the caretaker agreed without hesitation and even encouraged him to enjoy the breathtaking view of the Crystal Pool.

"So, this is the Crystal Pool?"

Towering rock walls encircled the entire pool, while the surrounding soil was an eerie pale color. From the cracks between the raised mounds, pungent, scalding vapors continuously rose into the air. Because of this, many Koffing had made their home here, feasting on the naturally abundant gases.

"Looks like Oni Mountain used to be a volcano. And at night, the Crystal Pool really does have a dreamlike beauty." Summer sighed in admiration.

Under the night sky, without the interference of sunlight, the Terastal crystals settled at the bottom of the pool shone even more brilliantly, radiating dazzling, multicolored luminescence. The scene was so surreal, it felt like stepping into a fantasy realm.

"Bo…"

Ogerpon was mesmerized as well. It had never climbed to the peak of Oni Mountain, nor had it ever witnessed the breathtaking beauty of the Crystal Pool. And with the hope of finally learning the whereabouts of the man it sought, its excitement only grew.

"Ogerpon, to uncover the truth… to find your precious companion… it's time to Terastallize."

Summer gave Ogerpon a firm nod. The night, the Crystal Pool, and the intense emotions—all the conditions needed to summon the mist were in place. Tonight, the secret of the Crystal Pool would finally be revealed!

"Gaaaooo!"

Ogerpon met his gaze with unwavering determination. Holding the Teal Mask in both hands, it solemnly placed it upon its face. At that moment, immense Terastal energy surged toward it, instantly attracting the Crystal Sprouts and Crystal Blooms that fed on the energy.

The dazzling Terastal crystals enveloped Ogerpon, and as it broke free from the shimmering encasement, the enormous mask let out a wild, echoing laugh. The essence of Ogerpon's emotions—pure euphoria—spread across the surroundings.

At the same time, a brilliant light erupted from the depths of the Crystal Pool. It was responding to Ogerpon's emotions!

"The mist is forming! Ogerpon, don't—Ogerpon?!" Summer called out, but all around him was nothing but thick, dense fog. He could no longer see anything or anyone.

"Bo-nioh!"

On the other side, Ogerpon panicked upon realizing that Summer had vanished. Fear surged in its heart once more—the fear of losing someone important. It felt that unbearable loneliness creeping in again, and it desperately didn't want to experience it ever again.

"You are… Ogerpon?"

A familiar voice drew Ogerpon's attention. The massive mask dissipated its Terastallization, falling gently back into its hands. Standing before it was a towering man—broad-shouldered, golden-haired, with a warm, kind face… though now marred by a fresh scar, giving him a slightly intimidating presence.

"It really is you. Looks like you triggered the Crystal Pool's mist… But unfortunately, this can only be a fleeting reunion." The man's voice carried deep regret.

"Gaaaooo…"

Beside the man, a crimson-scaled dragon let out a low growl, as if trying to comfort him. Its limbs were powerful, its razor-sharp claws seemingly capable of rending the very earth apart.

The creature's chest bore a large, dark, tire-like structure—the very source of its thunderous roars.

Its amber eyes exuded majesty, yet there was also a glimmer of curiosity within them. Its thick tail swayed with excitement.

The dragon's head was adorned with a feathered crest of deep blue, soft purple, and pure white, accompanied by two long tendrils of the same colors, as if declaring its dominance at the peak of power.

"Bo-nioh?!"

Ogerpon was visibly startled by the dragon's roar. Even though it had clearly lowered its volume, the sheer force behind the sound still carried an overwhelming sense of intimidation.

Noticing Ogerpon's reaction, the crimson dragon instinctively leaned its neck closer but quickly realized its mistake, pulling back in silence.

"Ogerpon… I'm sorry. I truly am. I left you all alone…" The man sighed. "Ah, you've taken such good care of the mask."

"Gao!"

Ogerpon sprinted forward and jumped up, punching the man square in the chest before bursting into tears.

Do you have any idea how much I suffered during that time?!

"Cough… Looks like you've gotten stronger." The man's pupils trembled slightly. "I know no words can truly make up for the pain I've caused you, but there's something I need to ask—Ogerpon, are you happy now?"

Ogerpon took a few steps back, wiped its tears away, and then beamed with a bright smile.

Yes, I'm happy!

Everyone in the Sunset Forest was kind and welcoming. Every day was filled with joy. And most importantly, Ogerpon had met someone just as important as him.

It was because of him that Ogerpon had escaped the abyss of loneliness.

Every moment spent with him was full of excitement and surprises, and he treated Ogerpon with kindness beyond measure.

"…I see." The man's expression softened with relief. Then, in a quieter voice, he asked, "So… this important person of yours… is he a man or a woman?"

"Bo!"

Ogerpon raised its tiny hand high. A man—a handsome man!

"…What?!" The man's mouth twitched, an odd sense of unease creeping over him. Why did this feel like a father losing his daughter to some random guy? "A brat, huh? No… I suppose I have no right to complain, given how unfit I've been as a guardian…"

He let out a deep sigh before kneeling down and gently patting Ogerpon's head.

"Ogerpon… may all your days be filled with health, happiness, and boundless energy—my dear daughter."

Then, with a teasing grin, he added, "Oh, and one more thing—if that guy ever dares to treat you poorly, no matter the distance—across space, time, or even different worlds—I will hunt him down."

"And to that unseen young man… thank you. From the bottom of my heart."

"Gaaaooo…"

The crimson dragon extended its claws and plucked a feather from its crest, infusing it with a brilliant, warm energy. Then, it knelt on one leg and carefully handed the feather to Ogerpon.

"Heh, if Koraidon's giving you a gift, then I can't just sit back and be stingy." The man took out a pink Terastal crystal and placed it in Ogerpon's free hand.

"Ogerpon, you should be able to use this now. This crystal carries my emotions… It is love. May it aid you on your journey ahead."

The figures of the man and the crimson dragon slowly blurred, fading into the mist.

With a gentle smile, the man bid farewell.

If this imperfect fatherly love can protect you, then I have no regrets.

Ogerpon held back its tears and returned his smile.

Goodbye, my beloved father. I promise—I will live a happy life!

"You are…? I didn't expect to meet again under these circumstances, Summer… Hmm, this scent… I see. So this time, it's Kitakami?"

Before him stood an elderly man, full of vitality despite his age. His long, waterfall-like white beard complemented his sharp, piercing eyes. Dressed in a robe embroidered with ancient patterns, he exuded an aura of authority.

"Who are you?" Summer asked in confusion. He had never seen this old man before.

"Ho… is that so? My name is Dunn. Or perhaps… you may know me better as the Dragon Emperor, Howard."

(End of Chapter)

# 228: Chapter 231: Answers

"The Dragon Emperor, Howard? Sorry, I've never heard of you."

Summer furrowed his brows in confusion. Dragon Emperor—the title sounded like that of a Dragon-type Trainer. But the surname Howard… he felt like he had heard it somewhere before.

"How odd. Just earlier, when you heard that title, your eyes were practically glowing with excitement." The old man stroked his beard.

The once-imposing elder had suddenly taken on a more carefree demeanor. But now wasn't the time for distractions—the real focus was on the mystery of the Crystal Pool. He seemed to recognize Kitakami, which meant he might know something.

"Master Howard, what exactly is hidden within the mist of the Crystal Pool? And why have we met here?" Summer asked, instinctively addressing him with respect.

"Master, huh? Hohoho! I never thought I'd hear you call me that." The old man chuckled. "Alright, let me tell you… You can think of the Crystal Pool as a teleportation point."

"A teleportation point?"

"That's right! A miracle of space and time, made possible by Terastal energy!"

"Wait… Does that mean the Crystal Pool has the power to traverse time and space? So Ogerpon's companion was transported to another timeline?" Summer muttered as he pieced things together.

"The next question is—why did I meet you here?" The old man continued, but then he smirked. "Before I answer, I have a question for you as well."

Since Howard had answered his question, it was only fair to return the courtesy. Summer nodded. "Go ahead."

"Hohoho… Summer, do you raise Dragon-type Pokémon?"

"Huh? Why that question? Well… I do have a Dragon-type Pokémon."

"Oh? And which one is it?" The old man asked with intrigue.

"It's a Jangmo-o."

"Hohoho! A Jangmo-o, eh? Not bad, that does suit you." The old man chuckled. "You're not spoiling it, are you?"

"Of course not! Jangmo-o is a natural-born warrior. It has to grow through battle. Besides, it works hard on its own—it even learned Dragon Tail, Dragon Claw, and X-Scissor all by itself." Summer protested.

"X-Scissor? Hohoho! Now that's a talented little one. I'm satisfied." Howard nodded approvingly, his figure gradually fading.

"Master Howard, my second question…"

"Oh? That's easy. Just ask your father when you get back. Hohoho!"

The old man let out a hearty laugh as he disappeared into the mist, leaving Summer utterly baffled.

My father?

But I transmigrated here! I don't even know who my father is in this world!

Feeling a headache coming on, Summer rubbed his temples and shook his head. At least one thing was certain now—the Crystal Pool did have the ability to transcend time and space. Howard was likely from another timeline.

"Bo-nioh!"

A familiar voice rang out. Summer suddenly felt something bump against his leg. Looking down, he saw Ogerpon gazing up at him, tears glistening in its eyes.

"Are you okay? What happened, Ogerpon? Did someone bully you?" Summer quickly crouched down to comfort it.

Ogerpon shook its head and presented the two items it had received from the man.

"A Terastal crystal… and this feather?"

[Feather of the Winged King – A feather from the ancient ruler of the land. On a clear day, it enhances the holder's strongest attribute.]

Hearing the explanation from his assistant, Summer was stunned. This was undoubtedly an incredibly powerful item, once belonging to a mighty ruler of the past. But… how had Ogerpon obtained it?

Seeing Summer's puzzled expression, Ogerpon waved its little hands excitedly and began recounting everything that had happened. It even had Hatterene relay the man's exact words.

"How could I possibly treat Ogerpon poorly? That old man is such a doting father… But one thing's for sure—he must really love you."

Summer looked at the pink Terastal crystal in his hand. Imprinted within it was the man's emotions—a father's love.

Ogerpon nodded firmly. Whether it was him, the folks in the Lush Forest, or Summer—they were all its dearest family.

"Ogerpon, can you Terastallize one more time? I want to test if the Crystal Pool's teleportation ability can be triggered again."

"Gaooo!"

Of course! Ogerpon activated its unique Terastal form once more—but this time, the Crystal Pool remained unresponsive.

"I thought so. A phenomenon like this must require time to recharge." Summer nodded. "Alright, you can cancel the Terastallization now. We should head back."

"Whether it's the spoils from defeating Pecharunt or the crystal from your father, they must have a greater purpose. Let's use them to create two brand-new masks!"

"Bo-nioh!"

Ogerpon's eyes sparkled with excitement. New masks? That sounds amazing! But then it hesitated—where would they find a skilled mask craftsman?

"Heh, Ogerpon, don't forget—I'm pretty good at carving. Leave the new masks to me!"

Three Days Later – The Home of Carmine and Kieran

After three days of dedicated work, the two new masks were finally in their final stage—embedding the Terastal crystals.

Though Summer was confident in his woodworking skills, he knew that craftsmanship of this level required experience. So, he sought the guidance of Grandpa Carl—Carmine and Kieran's grandfather, a master craftsman with generations of expertise.

Grandpa Carl approved of Summer's carving skills, but in other areas, he still had much to learn. To ensure the masks were of the highest quality, Summer handled the carving while Grandpa Carl took care of the finer details.

The first mask was named the Dread Poison Mask, modeled after Pecharunt's own terrified expression.The second mask was named the Phantom Moon Mask, inspired by Ogerpon's radiant smile.

To enhance their themes, Grandpa Carl incorporated additional elements—the Dread Poison Mask was adorned with a skull motif, representing the lethal nature of Poison-types, while the Phantom Moon Mask featured a crescent moon, symbolizing the mystical aura of Fairy-types.

The masks were crafted from Eternal Willow Wood, a rare tree species found only in the Eternal Forest. This wood was lightweight, durable, and highly resistant to decay.

For the coloring process, the paint had to be mixed with water from Kitakami's rivers and infused with Terastal shards. Since the masks represented Poison and Fairy types, Summer sought out Pokémon that could naturally Terastallize into those types.

In his pursuit of perfection, Summer hunted down every single Poison and Fairy Terastal Pokémon in Kitakami. It was said that for a long time after, no one could find a wild Poison or Fairy Terastal Pokémon in the area.

However, after all his effort, Grandpa Carl simply chuckled and told him, "You really didn't need that much." In the end, Summer was left with two entire sacks of excess Terastal shards.

The final step—embedding the Terastal crystals—was personally handled by Grandpa Carl. With his previous experience in mask restoration, the process went smoothly.

As the two newly completed masks lay before them, Ogerpon couldn't contain its joy. These were handcrafted by Summer himself—a testament to their new and unbreakable bond.

[New Items Unlocked: Dread Poison Mask (Poison/Fear), Phantom Moon Mask (Fairy/Love).]

The assistant's notification confirmed that Ogerpon could now Terastallize these masks, granting it two brand-new abilities—two new manifestations of its spirit.

"No time to waste. Let's find the perfect place to test them out. Ogerpon, let's witness your newfound power!"

(End of Chapter)
